# Third-party notices

Calcsta is an unofficial modified and simplified version of Stabileo, focused on
commonly used mechanical design features. Calcsta is licensed under the **GNU
Affero General Public License v3.0**; see the repository `LICENSE` or the
deployed `/LICENSE.txt`. Copyright in the original
Stabileo code remains with LambdaClass and the respective Stabileo contributors.
Its production artifacts also contain the third-party components listed below.

This file is GENERATED by `web/scripts/third-party-notices.mjs` and checked by
`third-party-notices.test.ts`. Edit the generator, not this file.

Only components that reach a **distributed runtime artifact** are listed. Build
and test tooling — Vite, Vitest, Playwright, TypeScript, Criterion — is omitted.
